[p3] ROWS RETAINED PER HORIZON (drop policy: no retained frame within +/-3 d of t+Delta inside the SAME cube -> drop the row; never wrap, never extrapolate)
[p3]   1580 retained frames, 115 cubes; 230 rows are ineligible for lack of 3 prior frames, leaving 1350 eligible t
[p3]    Delta   rows  cubes  cubes w/ none  % of eligible t   distinct target DOY
[p3]        5    510    115              0           37.8%                    29
[p3]       25    484    114              1           35.9%                    23
[p3]       50    445    115              0           33.0%                    23
[p3]      100    196     94             21           14.5%                    15
[p3]   median cube covers 135 days of retained frames, so the 100-day horizon is the one the window boundary bites: it is REPORTED here and carried on every row of the CSV as n_retained / n_cubes.
[p3]   distinct target DOY is printed because the proxy climatology is a 4-harmonic curve with 9 parameters: a horizon whose target frames land on few distinct days gives it little to fit, and the 100-day row is the thin one.
[p3]   retention SHRINKS as required: 510.0 rows at 5.0 d -> 196.0 at 100.0 d (62% lost to the window boundary), 94.0/115.0 cubes still contribute. The longest horizon is 74% of the median cube's 135-day window, so the requirement binds.
[p3]   0 intermediate step(s) rise rather than fall -- retention is not required to be monotone in between, because a horizon lands on the acquisition lattice or it does not.
[p3] proxy climatology is identifiable everywhere it is fitted: the thinnest case is spatial_block at Delta = 100 d with 12 distinct target days of year against 9 parameters. 12 for 9 is IDENTIFIED but THIN -- the longest-horizon climatology row is fitted on the least data in the table and should be read that way.

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).

  R2pooled uses every held-out prediction once and its interval is a delete-one-FOLD jackknife; R2/fold is the mean of the per-fold R-squareds and reads 'n/a' under loco, where a held-out cube contributes two to five rows and a per-fold R-squared is not a measurement. Margins are taken on R2pooled.
  medAE is the MEDIAN absolute error and top1% is the share of this row's held-out squared error carried by its worst 1% of rows. Read them together: an R-squared with top1% near 0.7 is a statement about a handful of rows (see print_target_outliers).
